                    b.outlined_text(p(O,T),q,j.parse_col(P,L.atCol[1]),L.atCol[2],w,L.atCol[2],c,false);
                else
                    b.text(p(O,T),q,j.parse_col(P,L.atCol[1]),L.atCol[2],c,false);
                end;
                k,g=Y,a;
            else
            end;
        else
        end;
        J,y=0,0;
        if L.name then
            z=P.plr.DisplayName;
            local Y,O=K.bounds(q,z);
            local K,T,a=(Z.tl.X+Z.tr.X)*0.5,(Z.tl.Y+Z.tr.Y)*0.5-O-R.Y,L.nOut and j.parse_col(P,L.nOCol,true)or nil;
            if L.nGrd then
                N(z,K,T,q,a,L.nCol[2],L.nGrdCol[1],L.nGrdCol[2],P.ph);
            elseif  a then
                b.outlined_text(p(K,T),q,j.parse_col(P,L.nCol[1]),L.nCol[2],a,L.nCol[2],z,true);
            else
                b.text(p(K,T),q,j.parse_col(P,L.nCol[1]),L.nCol[2],z,true);
            end;
            J,y=Y,O;
        else
        end;
        if L.av then
            local b,K,Y,O,T=q+6,(Z.tl.X+Z.tr.X)*0.5,(Z.tl.Y+Z.tr.Y)*0.5;
            if L.name and J>0 then
                O,T=K+J*0.5+b*0.5+4,Y-y*0.5-R.Y;
            else
                O,T=K,Y-b*0.5-R.Y;
            end;
            Y=P.plr;
            S.avatar(t(O),t(T),b,type(Y)=="table"and Y.avatar or Y.UserId);
        end;
        i,y,z=6,6,0;
        if X then
            if r=="left"then i+=A.X+3+0;
            i=if L.ht then
                i+(M.X+math.max(x,24))
            else
                i;
            else
                if r=="right"then y+=A.X+3+0;
                y=if L.ht then
                    y+(M.X+math.max(x,24))
                else
                    y;
                else
                    J=B.Y+4+0;
                    J=if L.ht then
                        J+I
                    else
                        J;
                        z=if J>z then
                            J
                        else
                            z;
                        end;
                    end;
                else
                end;
                if U then
                    if f=="left"then i+=A.X+3+h*7;
                    i=if L.at then
                        i+(M.X+math.max(k,24))
                    else
                        i;
                    else
                        if f=="right"then y+=A.X+3+h*7;
                        y=if L.at then
                            y+(M.X+math.max(k,24))
                        else
                            y;
                        else
                            J=B.Y+4+h*22;
                            J=if L.at then
                                J+g
                            else
                                J;
                                z=if J>z then
                                    J
                                else
                                    z;
                                end;
                            end;
                        else
                        end;
                        E.bx=(Z.bl.X+Z.br.X)*0.5;
                        E.by=(Z.bl.Y+Z.br.Y)*0.5+j.d_off.Y+z;
                        E.lx=Z.tl.X-i;
                        E.rx=Z.tr.X+y;
                        E.ly=Z.tl.Y;
                        E.ry=Z.tr.Y;
                        E.sb=0;
                        E.sl=0;
                        E.sr=0;
                        U,f,X=P.indicators,P.fr or false,d.place;
                        if U then
                            if L.bomb then
                                if not f then
                                    if U.bomb==true then
                                        X(P,"bomb",L.bombPos or"right",q,j.parse_col(P,L.bombCol[1]),L.bombCol[2],n,L.bombGrd,L.bombGrdCol[1],L.bombGrdCol[2]);
                                    end;
                                end;
                            end;
                            if L.scope then
                                if not f then
                                    if U.scope==true then
                                        X(P,"scope",L.scopePos or"right",q,j.parse_col(P,L.scopeCol[1]),L.scopeCol[2],n,L.scopeGrd,L.scopeGrdCol[1],L.scopeGrdCol[2]);
                                    end;
                                end;
                            end;
                            if L.flashed then
                                if U.flashed==true then
                                    X(P,"flashed",L.flashedPos or"right",q,j.parse_col(P,L.flashedCol[1]),L.flashedCol[2],n,L.flashedGrd,L.flashedGrdCol[1],L.flashedGrdCol[2]);
                                end;
                            end;
                            if L.defuse then
                                if not f then
                                    if U.defuse==true then
                                        X(P,"defuse",L.defusePos or"right",q,j.parse_col(P,L.defuseCol[1]),L.defuseCol[2],n,L.defuseGrd,L.defuseGrdCol[1],L.defuseGrdCol[2]);
                                    end;
                                end;
                            end;
                        end;
                        if L.dis then
                            X(P,G(P.dis).." studs",L.dPos or"bottom",q,j.parse_col(P,L.dCol[1]),L.dCol[2],L.dOut and j.parse_col(P,L.dOCol,true)or nil,L.dGrd,L.dGrdCol[1],L.dGrdCol[2]);
                        end;
                        if L.wep then
                            Z=P.wep;
                            if type(Z)=="string"then if Z~=""then X(P,Z,L.wPos or"bottom",q,j.parse_col(P,L.wCol[1]),L.wCol[2],L.wOut and j.parse_col(P,L.wOCol,true)or nil,L.wGrd,L.wGrdCol[1],L.wGrdCol[2]);
                        end;
                    end;
                end;
                if L.wepIcon then
                    J=o.of(P.wep);
                    if J then
                        U=L.wepIconCol;
                        d.place_icon(P,J,L.wepIconPos or"bottom",L.wepIconSz or 16,j.parse_col(P,U[1]),U[2]);
                    end;
                end;
                if L.mon then
                    X(P,"$"..t(P.mon),L.monPos or"right",q,j.parse_col(P,L.monCol[1]),L.monCol[2],n,L.monGrd,L.monGrdCol[1],L.monGrdCol[2]);
                end;
                if L.pn then
                    X(P,t(P.pn).."ms",L.pnPos or"right",q,j.parse_col(P,L.pnCol[1]),L.pnCol[2],n,L.pnGrd,L.pnGrdCol[1],L.pnGrdCol[2]);
                end;
                if L.skel then
                    if P.skpts then
                        q,f,J,X=P.skpts,j.parse_col(P,L.skelCol[1]),L.skelCol[2],d.bone;
                        X(P,f,J,q.hd,q.ur);
                        X(P,f,J,q.ur,q.lr);
                        X(P,f,J,q.ur,q.lua);
                        X(P,f,J,q.lua,q.lfa);
                        X(P,f,J,q.ur,q.rua);
                        X(P,f,J,q.rua,q.rfa);
                        X(P,f,J,q.lr,q.lul);
                        X(P,f,J,q.lul,q.lll);
                        X(P,f,J,q.lr,q.rul);
                        X(P,f,J,q.rul,q.rll);
                    end;
                end;
            end;
            return d;
        end;
        D.A=function()local j,h=D.load("b"),D.load("s");
        local b=D.load("x");
        local K=j.guiservice;
        local j=getgenv();
        local S=math.tan;
        local o=math.rad;
        local t=math.min;
        local G=table.clear;
        local i=CFrame.fromMatrix;
        local Y,O=CFrame.identity.PointToObjectSpace,{};
        O.__index=O;
        O.new=function(T,x)return setmetatable({plr=T,itf=x,ph=math.random(),char=nil,en=false,fr=false,onSc=false,dis=0,corn=nil,charCache={},cacheValid=false,links={},skpts=nil,dir=nil,viewport=nil,activeCam=nil,indicators=nil,hp=0,maxHp=100,arm=0,wep="",mon=0,pn=0,_hd=nil,_quiet=false,_cornT={},_cornPts={},_arSnap=true},O);
    end;
    O.project=function(T,x)local p,A=T.viewport,T.activeCam;
    if p then
        T=p.AbsoluteSize;
        if T.X<1 then
            return Vector2.zero,false,0;
        elseif  T.Y<1 then
            return Vector2.zero,false,0;
        end;
        local M=A.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(x);
        if M.Z>-0.05 then
            return Vector2.zero,false,0;
        end;
        local a,B=S(o((A.FieldOfView or 70)*0.5)),-M.Z;
        local S,o,N=M.X/(B*a*(T.X/T.Y)),M.Y/(B*a),p.AbsolutePosition+K:GetGuiInset();
        return Vector2.new(N.X+T.X*(0.5+S*0.5),N.Y+T.Y*(0.5-o*0.5)),true,B;
    end;
    if h.overridden()then
        return h.project_view(x);
    end;
    local K,S=A:WorldToViewportPoint(x);
    return Vector2.new(K.X,K.Y),S,K.Z;
end;
O.drop_events=function(K)local S=K.links;
local K,o=S.added,S.removed;
if K then
    pcall(K.Disconnect,K);
    S.added=nil;
end;
if o then
    pcall(o.Disconnect,o);
    S.removed=nil;
end;
end;
local function K(S,o)if h.body_parts[o.Name]then
    S.cacheValid=false;
end;
end;
O.watch=function(S,o)S:drop_events();
if not(o)then
    return;
end;
local T=S.links;
T.added=o.ChildAdded:Connect(function(x)K(S,x);
end);
T.removed=o.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(o)K(S,o);
end);
end;
O.validate=function(K)local S=K.char;
if S then
    if S.Parent then
        if K.cacheValid==false then
            return false;
        end;
    else
        return false;
    end;
else
    return false;
end;
local o=K.charCache;
K=#o;
if K==0 then
    return false;
end;
for T=1,t(3,K),1 do
    local K=o[T];
    if K then
        if K.Parent then
            if K.Parent~=S then
                return false;
            end;
        else
            return false;
        end;
    else
        return false;
    end;
end;
return true;
end;
O.rebuild=function(K)local S=K.charCache;
if K.char then
    if not(K.char.Parent)then
        G(S);
        return;
    end;
else
    G(S);
    return;
end;
h.char_parts(K.char,S);
K.cacheValid=true;
end;
O.update=function(K)local S=K.itf;
local o=b.shared_for(S,K.plr);
local t=o.opt;
K.opt=t;
K.indicators=nil;
K._quiet=false;
local T=K.plr;
if if T then
    not K.plr.Parent
else
    not T then
        K.en=false;
        K.char=nil;
        K.onSc=false;
        G(K.charCache);
        K.cacheValid=false;
        K.skpts=nil;
        K._hd=nil;
        K:drop_events();
        return;
    end;
    T=o.char;
    if T~=K.char then
        K.char=T;
        G(K.charCache);
        K.cacheValid=false;
        K.cacheChar=nil;
        K.skpts=nil;
        K.dir=nil;
        K._hd=nil;
        K._arSnap=true;
        K:watch(T);
    end;
    K.en=o.en;
    K.fr=o.fr;
    if not(K.en)then
        K.onSc=false;
        K.cacheValid=false;
        K.skpts=nil;
        return;
    end;
    T=K._hd;
    if if T then
        T.Parent~=K.char
    else
        not T then
            T=K.char and K.char:FindFirstChild("Head")or nil;
            K._hd=T;
        else
        end;
        if if T then
            not T.Parent
        else
            not T then
                G(K.charCache);
                K.cacheValid=false;
                K.skpts=nil;
                K.onSc=false;
                return;
            end;
            local x,p=h.camera(),o.clone;
            local A=p and p.viewport;
            if A then
                if not(rawget(j,"VISUALS_TAB_OPEN"))then
                    K.onSc=false;
                    return;
                end;
                x=A.CurrentCamera or x;
            else
            end;
            K.viewport=A;
            K.activeCam=x;
            local j,M,a=K:project(T.Position);
            local B=S.shSet;
            local N=B.limDis and a>B.maxDis;
            K.onSc=M and not N;
            K.dis=((h.overridden()and h.view_frame().Position or x.CFrame.Position)-T.Position).Magnitude;
            M=h.viewport();
            if A then
            elseif  N then
            elseif  a>0.05 then
                B=(h.rt.foc or 800)*8/a;
                if j.X>-B then
                    if j.Y>-B then
                        if j.X<M.X+B then
                            if j.Y<M.Y+B then
                                o.vis=true;
                            end;
                        end;
                    end;
                end;
            end;
            if K.onSc then
                o=b.opt_flags(S,t);
                if o.hp then
                    K.hp,K.maxHp=S.getHp(K.plr,K.char);
                end;
                if o.arm then
                    K.arm=S.getArm(K.plr);
                end;
                if o.wep then
                    K.wep=S.getWep(K.plr);
                end;
                if o.mon then
                    K.mon=S.getMoney and S.getMoney(K.plr)or 0;
                end;
                if o.pn then
                    K.pn=S.getPing and S.getPing(K.plr)or 0;
                end;
                if o.ind then
                    if type(S.getIndicators)=="function"then K.indicators=S.getIndicators(K.plr,K.char,t.flashed==true);
                end;
            end;
            if t.skel then
                K:skeleton();
            else
                K.skpts=nil;
            end;
            if not(o.corn)then
                K.corn=nil;
                K._quiet=not t.skel;
                return;
            end;
            if#K.charCache==0 then
                K:rebuild();
            elseif  not(K.cacheValid)then
                K:rebuild();
            end;
            if#K.charCache==0 then
                K.onSc=false;
                return;
            end;
            N,M=h.bounds(K.charCache);
            if N then
                if not M then
                    K.onSc=false;
                    K.cacheValid=false;
                    return;
                end;
            else
                K.onSc=false;
                K.cacheValid=false;
                return;
            end;
            if A then
                K.corn=h.calc_corners_viewport(N,M,x,A);
            else
                K.corn=h.calc_corners(N,M,K._cornT,K._cornPts);
            end;
            return;
        end;
        if t.offAr then
            if not(p)then
                M=j-h.viewport()*0.5;
                M=if a<=0 then
                    -M
                else
                    M;
                    S=M.Magnitude;
                    if S>0.001 then
                        K.dir=M/S;
                        return;
                    end;
                    o=h.overridden()and h.view_frame()or x.CFrame;
                    B=Y(i(o.Position,o.RightVector,Vector3.yAxis),T.Position);
                    A=Vector2.new(B.X,B.Z);
                    N=A.Magnitude;
                    K.dir=N>0.001 and A/N or K.dir or Vector2.new(0,-1);
                end;
            end;
        end;
        O.skeleton=function(j)local h=j.char;
        if h then
            if not(j.onSc)then
                j.skpts=nil;
                return;
            end;
        else
            j.skpts=nil;
            return;
        end;
        local K=j._skparts;
        if K then
        else
            K={};
            j._skparts=K;
        end;
        if j._skchar~=h or not j.cacheValid then
            local S=b.find_part;
            j._skchar=h;
            K.hd=S(h,"Head");
            K.ur=S(h,"UpperTorso")or S(h,"Torso");
            K.lr=S(h,"LowerTorso")or S(h,"Torso");
            K.lua=S(h,"LeftUpperArm")or S(h,"Left Arm");
            K.rua=S(h,"RightUpperArm")or S(h,"Right Arm");
            K.lfa=S(h,"LeftLowerArm")or S(h,"LeftHand");
            K.rfa=S(h,"RightLowerArm")or S(h,"RightHand");
            K.lul=S(h,"LeftUpperLeg")or S(h,"Left Leg");
            K.rul=S(h,"RightUpperLeg")or S(h,"Right Leg");
            K.lll=S(h,"LeftLowerLeg")or S(h,"LeftFoot");
            K.rll=S(h,"RightLowerLeg")or S(h,"RightFoot");
        end;
        h=K.hd;
        if h then
            if not(h.Parent)then
                j.skpts=nil;
                return;
            end;
        else
            j.skpts=nil;
            return;
        end;
        local b=j._skpts;
        if b then
        else
            b={};
            j._skpts=b;
        end;
        G(b);
        b.hd=h.Position;
        local h,S,o,t,i,Y,T,x,p,A=K.ur,K.lr,K.lua,K.rua,K.lfa,K.rfa,K.lul,K.rul,K.lll,K.rll;
        if h then
            if h.Parent then
                b.ur=h.Position;
            end;
        end;
        if S then
            if S.Parent then
                b.lr=S.Position;
            end;
        end;
        if o then
            if o.Parent then
                b.lua=o.Position;
            end;
        end;
        if t then
            if t.Parent then
                b.rua=t.Position;
            end;
        end;
        if i then
            if i.Parent then
                b.lfa=i.Position;
            end;
        end;
        if Y then
            if Y.Parent then
                b.rfa=Y.Position;
            end;
        end;
        if T then
            if T.Parent then
                b.lul=T.Position;
            end;
        end;
        if x then
            if x.Parent then
                b.rul=x.Position;
            end;
        end;
        if p then
            if p.Parent then
                b.lll=p.Position;
            end;
        end;
        if A then
            if A.Parent then
                b.rll=A.Position;
            end;
        end;
        j.skpts=b;
    end;
    O.destroy=function(j)j:drop_events();
    if j.av then
        pcall(j.av.Destroy,j.av);
        j.av=nil;
    end;
    G(j.charCache);
    j.corn=nil;
    j.skpts=nil;
    j.char=nil;
    j._hd=nil;
    j.viewport=nil;
    j.activeCam=nil;
    j.indicators=nil;
end;
return O;
end;
D.B=function()local j=D.load("b");
local h=D.load("a");
local b=D.load("s");
local K=D.load("x");
local S=D.load("p");
local o=j.workspace;
local t=j.lp;
local G=b.white;
local i=math.min;
local Y=math.max;
local O,T,x=Vector3.new,{["Counter-Terrorists"]=true,CT=true},{};
x.UpperTorso=O(1,1.04,1);
x.LowerTorso=O(0.95,1.5,1);
x.Torso=O(1,1,1);
local p=Enum.AdornShading and Enum.AdornShading.XRay or nil;
local A=Enum.AdornCullingMode and Enum.AdornCullingMode.Never or nil;
if type(setfflag)=="function"then pcall(setfflag,"AdornShadingAPI","True");
end;
local M,a;
local function B()local N=o.CurrentCamera;
if not(N)then
    return nil;
end;
if if M then
    not M.Parent
else
    not M then
        M=Instance.new("Folder");
        M.Parent=N;
        h.own(M);
        a=Instance.new("Part");
        local h=a;
        h.Size=Vector3.one;
        a.Anchored=true;
        a.CanCollide=false;
        a.CanQuery=false;
        a.CanTouch=false;
        a.CastShadow=false;
        a.Massless=true;
        a.Locked=true;
        a.Transparency=1;
        a.Parent=M;
    elseif  M.Parent~=N then
        M.Parent=N;
    end;
    N=M;
    return N;
end;
local h=Vector3.one*0.004;
local N=CFrame.new();
local I,R,n,E=Vector3.zero,false,0,{};
E.__index=E;
E.begin_frame=function(d)local w=o.CurrentCamera;
if not(w)then
    return false;
end;
if not(B())then
    return false;
end;
local o=w.CFrame;
a.CFrame=o;
N=o:Inverse();
I=o.Position;
n+=d or 0;
R=n>=0.6;
if R then
    n=0;
end;
return true;
end;
local function o(B)local n=tonumber(B.chamsGlow)or 4;
return if n<1 then
    1
else
    n;
end;
local function B(n,d)return Color3.new(n.R*d,n.G*d,n.B*d);
end;
local n=b.body_parts;
local function d(w,P)if w.Parent~=P then
    return false;
elseif  not(n[w.Name])then
    return false;
end;
if not(w:IsA("BasePart"))then
    return false;
end;
P=w.Size;
return P.X>0.05 and P.Y>0.05 and P.Z>0.05;
end;
local function n(w)local P=w:FindFirstChildOfClass("SpecialMesh");
if P then
    w=P.Scale;
    if typeof(w)=="Vector3"then return w;
end;
end;
return nil;
end;
local function w(P,r)local L,Z=P.Size,P.Name;
local X=L;
if Z=="Head"then local q=n(P);
L=if q then
    X*q
else
    X;
    return if r~=1 then
        L*r
    else
        L;
    end;
    L=n(P);
    if L then
        return X*L;
    end;
    r=x[Z];
    if r then
        return X*r;
    end;
    Z,r,P=X.X,X.Y,X.Z;
    if r>=Z then
        if r>=P then
            return O(Z*1,r*1.08,P*1);
        end;
    end;
    if Z>=r then
        if Z>=P then
            return O(Z*1.08,r*1,P*1);
        end;
    end;
    return O(Z*1,r*1,P*1.08);
end;
E.new=function(O,x)return setmetatable({plr=O,itf=x,parts={},slots=j.weak(),links={},charRef=nil,shown=false,armed=false,fill_col=nil,glow_col=nil,head_trim=1},E);
end;
E.drop_part=function(j,O)local x=j.slots[O];
if not(x)then
    return;
end;
j.slots[O]=nil;
if x.glow then
    pcall(x.glow.Destroy,x.glow);
end;
if x.fill then
    pcall(x.fill.Destroy,x.fill);
end;
end;
local function j(O)local x=Instance.new("BoxHandleAdornment");
x.Adornee=a;
x.Transparency=0;
x.ZIndex=O;
x.Visible=false;
if p then
    x.Shading=p;
end;
if A then
    x.AdornCullingMode=A;
end;
x.Parent=M;
return x;
end;
E.add_part=function(O,x)if O.slots[x]then
    return;
elseif  not(d(x,O.charRef))then
    return;
end;
local p={glow=nil,fill=nil,base=w(x,O.head_trim),size=x.Size};
if O.armed then
    local A,a=j(0),j(1);
    A.Color3=O.glow_col or G;
    a.Color3=O.fill_col or G;
    A.Visible=O.shown;
    a.Visible=O.shown;
    p.glow=A;
    p.fill=a;
    O.fade=nil;
end;
local A=#O.parts+1;
O.parts[A]=x;
O.slots[x]=p;
end;
E.arm=function(O)if O.armed then
    return;
end;
O.armed=true;
local x,p,A,a=O.parts,O.slots,O.fill_col or G,O.glow_col or G;
for n=1,#x,1 do
    O=p[x[n]];
    if O then
        if not(O.fill)then
            local x,p=j(0),j(1);
            x.Color3=a;
            p.Color3=A;
            x.Visible=false;
            p.Visible=false;
            O.glow=x;
            O.fill=p;
        end;
    end;
end;
end;
E.clear=function(j)local O=j.links;
for x=#O,1,-1 do
    pcall(O[x].Disconnect,O[x]);
    O[x]=nil;
end;
O=j.parts;
for x=#O,1,-1 do
    j:drop_part(O[x]);
    O[x]=nil;
end;
table.clear(j.slots);
j.charRef=nil;
j.shown=false;
j.armed=false;
j.fade=nil;
end;
E.bind=function(j,O)j:clear();
if not O or not O.Parent then
    return;
end;
j.charRef=O;
j.head_trim=T[j.itf.getTeam(j.plr)]and 0.88 or 1;
local T=O:GetChildren();
for x=1,#T,1 do
    j:add_part(T[x]);
end;
T=j.links;
T[#T+1]=O.ChildAdded:Connect(function(x)if j.charRef==O then
    j:add_part(x);
end;
end);
T[#T+1]=O.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(T)if j.charRef==O then
    j:drop_part(T);
end;
end);
end;
E.rescan=function(j)if not(j.charRef)then
    return;
end;
local O,T,x=j.parts,j.slots,0;
for p=1,#O,1 do
    local A=O[p];
    p=A and T[A];
    if A then
        if A.Parent then
            if p then
                local T=x+1;
                O[T]=A;
                local a=A.Size;
                if p.size~=a then
                    p.size=a;
                    p.base=w(A,j.head_trim);
                end;
                x=T;
            else
                if A then
                    j:drop_part(A);
                end;
            end;
        else
            if A then
                j:drop_part(A);
            end;
        end;
    else
        if A then
            j:drop_part(A);
        end;
    end;
end;
for j=#O,x+1,-1 do
    O[j]=nil;
end;
end;
E.set_shown=function(j,O)j.shown=O;
local T,x=j.parts,j.slots;
for p=1,#T,1 do
    j=x[T[p]];
    if j then
        if j.fill then
            j.glow.Visible=O;
            j.fill.Visible=O;
        end;
    end;
end;
end;
E.recolor=function(j,O,T)j.fill_col=O;
j.glow_col=T;
local x,p=j.parts,j.slots;
for A=1,#x,1 do
    j=p[x[A]];
    if j then
        if j.fill then
            j.fill.Color3=O;
            j.glow.Color3=T;
        end;
    end;
end;
end;
E.update=function(j)local O=j.itf;
local T=K.shared_for(O,j.plr);
local K,x=T.opt,T.char;
local p=T.en and K and K.chams and x and x.Parent and j.plr~=t;
if if p then
    not M
else
    not p then
        if j.charRef then
            j:clear();
        end;
        return;
    end;
    if j.charRef~=x then
        j:bind(x);
    end;
    if R then
        j:rescan();
    end;
    T,p=j.parts,j.slots;
    local t,A=#T;
    for M=1,t,1 do
        x=T[M];
        if x then
            if x.Parent then
                A=x;
                break;
            end;
        end;
    end;
    x=A~=nil;
    if x then
        local M=O.shSet;
        local a=tonumber(M.maxDis);
        if a then
            if M.limDis then
                local M=I;
                x=if(M-A.Position).Magnitude>a then
                    false
                else
                    x;
                else
                end;
            else
            end;
        else
        end;
        if not x then
            if j.shown then
                j:set_shown(false);
            end;
            return;
        end;
        if not(j.armed)then
            j:arm();
        end;
        if not(j.shown)then
            j:set_shown(true);
        end;
        O=b.parse_col(j,K.chamsOCol[1])or G;
        x=B(O,o(K));
        if j.fill_col~=O then
            j:recolor(O,x);
        elseif  j.glow_col~=x then
            j:recolor(O,x);
        end;
        K=S.fade(j.plr);
        if j.fade~=K then
            j.fade=K;
            x=1-K;
            for b=1,t,1 do
                O=p[T[b]];
                if O then
                    if O.fill then
                        O.fill.Transparency=x;
                        O.glow.Transparency=x;
                    end;
                end;
            end;
        end;
        for b=1,t,1 do
            K=T[b];
            x=K and p[K];
            if x then
                if K.Parent then
                    j=N;
                    b=j*K.CFrame;
                    O=b.Position;
                    local j=i(0.6/Y(O.Magnitude,0.001),1);
                    local K,S,o=CFrame.new(O*j)*(b-O),(x.base*j):Max(h),x.fill;
                    o.CFrame=K;
                    o.Size=S;
                    j=x.glow;
                    j.CFrame=K;
                    j.Size=S*1.12;
                end;
            end;
        end;
    end;
    E.destroy=function(j)j:clear();
end;
return E;
end;
D.C=function()local j,h=D.load("b"),D.load("s");
local b=D.load("x");
local K=j.lp;
local j=h.white;
local S,o=Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop,{};
o.__index=o;
o.new=function(t,G)return setmetatable({plr=t,itf=G,inst=nil,char=nil,root=nil,links={},dirty=false,on=false,fc=nil,oc=nil,ft=nil,ot=nil},o);
end;
o.drop_links=function(t)local G=t.links;
for i=#G,1,-1 do
    t=G[i];
    G[i]=nil;
    pcall(t.Disconnect,t);
end;
end;
o.watch=function(t,G)t:drop_links();
if not(G)then
    return;
end;
local i=t.links;
local function Y()t.dirty=true;
end;
i[1]=G.ChildAdded:Connect(Y);
i[2]=G.ChildRemoved:Connect(Y);
end;
o.hide=function(t)local G=t.inst;
if t.on then
    t.on=false;
    if G then
        G.Enabled=false;
    end;
elseif  G then
    if G.Enabled then
        t.on=false;
        if G then
            G.Enabled=false;
        end;
    end;
end;
end;
o.unbind=function(t)t:hide();
t:drop_links();
t.char=nil;
t.root=nil;
t.dirty=false;
local G=t.inst;
if G then
    G.Adornee=nil;
end;
end;
o.ensure=function(t)local G,i=t.inst,h.holder;
if G then
    if G.Parent==i then
        return G;
    end;
end;
if G then
    pcall(G.Destroy,G);
end;
G=Instance.new("Highlight");
G.Enabled=false;
G.DepthMode=S;
G.Parent=i;
t.inst=G;
t.char=nil;
t.on=false;
t.fc=nil;
t.oc=nil;
t.ft=nil;
t.ot=nil;
return G;
end;
o.update=function(S)local t=S.itf;
local G=b.shared_for(t,S.plr);
local b,i,Y=G.opt,G.char,G.en;
if(if Y then
    not b
else
    not Y)or not b.hlt or not i or not i.Parent or S.plr==K then
        if S.char then
            S:unbind();
        end;
        return;
    end;
    Y,G=S:ensure(),S.root;
    if S.char~=i then
        S.char=i;
        G=i:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")or i:FindFirstChild("Head");
        S.root=G;
        S.dirty=false;
        S:watch(i);
        Y.Adornee=i;
    else
        if if G then
            G.Parent~=i
        else
            not G then
                G=i:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")or i:FindFirstChild("Head");
                S.root=G;
                S.dirty=G~=nil;
            else
            end;
            if S.dirty or Y.Adornee~=i then
                S.dirty=false;
                Y.Adornee=nil;
                Y.Adornee=i;
                S.on=false;
            end;
        end;
        if not G then
            S:hide();
            return;
        end;
        i=t.shSet;
        if i.limDis then
            t=tonumber(i.maxDis);
            if t then
                if(h.view_frame().Position-G.Position).Magnitude>t then
                    S:hide();
                    return;
                end;
            end;
        end;
        G,t=b.hltFCol,b.hltOCol;
        local b,K,i,O=h.parse_col(S,G[1])or j,h.parse_col(S,t[1])or j,1-(tonumber(G[2])or 0.5),1-(tonumber(t[2])or 1);
        if S.fc~=b then
            S.fc=b;
            Y.FillColor=b;
        end;
        if S.oc~=K then
            S.oc=K;
            Y.OutlineColor=K;
        end;
        if S.ft~=i then
            S.ft=i;
            Y.FillTransparency=i;
        end;
        if S.ot~=O then
            S.ot=O;
            Y.OutlineTransparency=O;
        end;
        if S.on then
            if not(Y.Enabled)then
                S.on=true;
                Y.Enabled=true;
            end;
        else
            S.on=true;
            Y.Enabled=true;
        end;
    end;
    o.destroy=function(j)j:drop_links();
    local h=j.inst;
    j.inst=nil;
    j.char=nil;
    j.root=nil;
    j.dirty=false;
    j.on=false;
    if h then
        pcall(h.Destroy,h);
    end;
end;
return o;
end;
D.D=function()local j=D.load("s");
local h=D.load("v");
local b=D.load("z");
local K=j.white;
local S=Color3.new();
local o,t=math.round,{};
t.__index=t;
t.new=function(G,i)i=assert(i,"No options");
i.en=i.en==nil and true or i.en;
i.txt=i.txt or"{name}";
i.txtCol=i.txtCol or{K,1};
i.txtOut=i.txtOut==nil and true or i.txtOut;
i.txtOCol=i.txtOCol or S;
i.txtSz=i.txtSz or 13;
i.txtFnt=i.txtFnt or 3;
i.limDis=i.limDis or false;
i.maxDis=i.maxDis or 150;
i.txtGrd=i.txtGrd or false;
i.txtGrdCol=i.txtGrdCol or{K,Color3.new(1,0,0)};
return setmetatable({inst=assert(G,"No instance"),opt=i,ph=math.random(),dead=false},t);
end;
t.update=function(K)end;
t.draw=function(K)if K.dead then
    return;
end;
local S=K.inst;
if S then
    if not(S.Parent)then
        K.dead=true;
        return;
    end;
else
    K.dead=true;
    return;
end;
local G=K.opt;
if not(G.en)then
    return;
end;
local i,Y=pcall(S.GetPivot,S);
if not(i)then
    return;
end;
i=Y.Position;
local Y,O,T=j.to_screen(i);
if Y then
    if not(O)then
        return;
    end;
else
    return;
end;
if G.limDis then
    if T>G.maxDis then
        return;
    end;
end;

                    else
                        if J==1 and U>1 then
                            f,k=p,(X[2]-y)/3;
                        else
                            if J==U and U>1 then
                                f,k=(X[U-1]-y)/3,p;
                            else
                                if U>2 then
                                    local z,g=X[J-1],X[J+1];
                                    local X,Q=(g-z)/6,o((y-z).Magnitude,(g-y).Magnitude)*0.42;
                                    X=if X.Magnitude>Q then
                                        if X.Magnitude>0 then
                                            X.Unit*Q
                                        else
                                            X
                                        else
                                            X;
                                            f,k=-X,X;
                                        else
                                            f,k=p,p;
                                        end;
                                    end;
                                end;
                            end;
                            L[J]=Path2DControlPoint.new(UDim2.fromOffset(y.X,y.Y),UDim2.fromOffset(f.X,f.Y),UDim2.fromOffset(k.X,k.Y));
                        end;
                        for X=#L,U+1,-1 do
                            L[X]=nil;
                        end;
                        return L;
                    end;
                    local function X(U)if U then
                        if not(U.label)then
                            return true;
                        end;
                    else
                        return true;
                    end;
                    U.label.Visible=false;
                    return true;
                end;
                local function U(z)if not(z)then
                    return true;
                end;
                local J=z.banks;
                for z=1,#J,1 do
                    X(J[z]);
                end;
                return true;
            end;
            local function z(J)if not(J)then
                return true;
            end;
            local y,f=J.groups,J.paths;
            for k=1,#y,1 do
                J=f[k];
                if J then
                    J.Visible=false;
                end;
                local J=y[k];
                if J then
                    J.Visible=false;
                end;
            end;
            return true;
        end;
        local function J(y)if not(y)then
            return true;
        end;
        local f=y.banks;
        for y=1,#f,1 do
            z(f[y]);
        end;
        return true;
    end;
    local function y(f)if not(f)then
        return true;
    end;
    local k=f.frames;
    for g=1,#k,1 do
        f=k[g];
        if f then
            f.Visible=false;
        end;
    end;
    return true;
end;

        _(H.banks[s]);

local function H(_)local s,F,v=_.fills,_.strokes,_.points;

    c(s[u],X);
end;

    c(F[s],z);

    c(v[s],y);

end;
local function c(_,s,F)local v=_.pending;

    return;
end;
if v==0 then
    F(_);
    _.active=0;
else
    local F,u=_.banks[v],_.banks[_.active];
    if u then
        if u~=F then
            s(u);
        end;
    end;
    _.active=v;
end;
_.pending=nil;
end;
local function _(s)local F,v,u,V=s.fills,s.strokes,s.points,true;

    local l=F[e];

        V=false;
        break;
    end;
end;
if V then
    for k=1,#v,1 do
        local e=v[k];
        k=e.pending;
        if type(k)=="number"and k>0 and not g(e.banks[k])then
            V=false;
            break;
        end;
    end;
else
end;
if V then
    for k=1,#u,1 do
        local g=u[k];
        k=g.pending;
        if type(k)=="number"and k>0 and not Q(g.banks[k])then
            V=false;
            break;
        end;
    end;
    if not(V)then
        H(s);
        return false;
    end;
else
    H(s);
    return false;
end;

    c(F[k],X,U);

    c(v[k],z,J);

    c(u[k],y,f);
end;

return true;
end;

local function g()local Q,c,s,F,v,u,V,e=k.bank,k.style,k.color,k.closed,k.width,k.opacity,k.base,k.controls;

    local mF,DF,jF;
    if c=="trail"then mF,DF=W==1 and 5.4 or W==2 and 2.7 or 1.25,W==1 and 0.26 or W==2 and 0.72 or 1;
    Q=W==1 and 0.12 or W==2 and 0.42;
    jF=Q or 0.78;

local function z(k,g,Q,c,s,F,v,u,V)local e,l=q(g,Q);
if l<(Q and 3 or 2)then
    return true;
end;
if type(F)~="table"then return Z(k,e,l,Q,c,s,u,V);
elseif #F==0 then
    return Z(k,e,l,Q,c,s,u,V);
end;
local C,W=0;
if not(Q)then
    W=C;
    for mF=1,l-1,1 do
        local DF,jF=e[mF],e[mF+1];
        if r(DF,jF,F,v)then
            if W>=2 then
                Z(k,K,W,false,c,s,u,V);
            end;
            W=0;
        else
            if W==0 then
                g=W+1;
                K[g]=DF;
                W=g;
            else
                if(DF-K[W]).Magnitude>0.1 then
                    mF=W+1;
                    K[mF]=DF;
                    W=mF;
                else
                end;
            end;
            if(jF-K[W]).Magnitude>0.1 then
                W+=1;
                K[W]=jF;
            else
            end;
        end;
    end;
    if W>=2 then
        Z(k,K,W,false,c,s,u,V);
    end;
    return true;
end;
Q=nil;
for mF=1,l,1 do
    g=mF==l and 1 or mF+1;
    if r(e[mF],e[g],F,v)then
        Q=mF;
        break;
    end;
end;
if not Q then
    return Z(k,e,l,true,c,s,u,V);
end;
W=C;
for mF=1,l,1 do
    g=(Q+mF-1)%l+1;
    mF,C=g==l and 1 or g+1,e[g];
    local g=e[mF];
    if r(C,g,F,v)then
        if W>=2 then
            Z(k,K,W,false,c,s,u,V);
        end;
        W=0;
    else
        if W==0 then
            mF=W+1;
            K[mF]=C;
            W=mF;
        else
            if(C-K[W]).Magnitude>0.1 then
                local Q=W+1;
                K[Q]=C;
                W=Q;
            else
            end;
        end;
        if(g-K[W]).Magnitude>0.1 then
            W+=1;
            K[W]=g;
        else
        end;
    end;
end;

    Z(k,K,W,false,c,s,u,V);
end;
return true;
end;

K.finish=function(j)local S=a[j];

    local i=o[1];

            i.misses=(i.misses or 0)+1;

                i.pending=0;
            else
                i.pending=nil;
            end;
        end;
    end;
    for i=2,#o,1 do
        o[i].pending=0;
    end;

        o[i].pending=0;
    end;

    return;

local function G(O,T,y)local f=t:Raycast(O+U,q,Z);

    f.Position+f.Normal*0.06

            end;

                return nil;

        else
            return nil;
        end;

        if T.Class~="Grenade"then return nil;

    end;

    return nil;
end;

end;

local function A()if H then
    if F then
        if n(H)then
            local r=H;
            return v(r);
        end;
    elseif  k(H)then
        local r=H;
        return a(r),nil;
    end;
    H=nil;
end;
local r=Y:GetTagged("Bomb");
local V=#r;
for l=1,V,1 do
    local jF=r[l];
    if n(jF)then
        local n,l=v(jF);
        if n then
            H,F=jF,true;
            return n,l;
        end;
    end;
end;
V=Y:GetTagged("WeaponDropped");
r=#V;
for n=1,r,1 do
    local r=V[n];
    if k(r)then
        n=a(r);
        if n then
            H,F=r,false;
            return n,nil;
        end;
    end;
end;
return nil,nil;
end;

        local O=J[G];

    end;

end;
end;
end;

local function K(t,Y,n)Q=o.sync();

    local o=b(n,t-Y*E);

        return o and 1 or-1;
    end;

        return 0;
    end;
    o=b(n,t-Y*d);
    if o~=b(n,t+Y*d)then
        return o and 1 or-1;
    end;
end;
n=not b(t,t-Y*w);

    return n and-1 or 1;
end;
return nil;
end;

end;

for u=0,#Q,1 do
    F=u+1;
    c[F]=u;
    H[F]=((u==0 and X or Q[u])-X).Magnitude;
end;
for u=2,f,1 do
    local V,e,l=c[u],H[u],u-1;
    while l>=1 and H[l]>e do
        c[l+1]=c[l];
        H[l+1]=H[l];
        l-=1;
    end;
    c[l+1]=V;
    H[l+1]=e;
end;
local u,V,e;
for l=0,1,1 do
    v=l==1;
    local C,W=v and 0.25 or 0,_(s,n,v);
    for s=1,f,1 do
        F=c[s];
        if l~=0 or F~=0 then
            local f=H[s]+C;
            if u then
                if f>=u then
                    break;
                end;
            end;
            local c=F==0 and X or Q[F];
            local X,Q,Q=P(L,c,q,U,W);
            if X then
                if A(L,L.state,X,Q)then
                    u,V,e=f,c,v;
                    break;
                end;
            end;
        end;
    end;
end;
return V,e;
end;
local function A(L,X)local q,U=p(X),x(X);
local f,Q=U*L.X+q*L.Y,-q*L.X+U*L.Y;
X=f*f+Q*Q;
if X>1 then
    U=1/O(X);
    f,Q=f*U,Q*U;
else
end;
return Vector3.new(f,0,Q);
end;
local function O(L,X)local q,U=p(X),x(X);
return Vector2.new(U*L.X-q*L.Z,q*L.X+U*L.Z);
end;
local function L(X,q,U,f)local Q=Vector3.new(U.X,0,U.Z);
local c,H=Q.Magnitude,d.strafe_yaw;
d.strafe_yaw=q;
if c<=0.05 then
    if X.Magnitude>0.001 then
        return X.Magnitude>1 and X.Unit or X;
    end;
    U=d.strafe_side or 1;
    d.strafe_side=U;
    return Vector2.new(U,0);
end;
U=Q/c;
Q=Vector3.new(U.Z,0,-U.X);
c=A(X,q):Dot(Q);
X=nil;
if T(c)>0.01 then
    X=c>0 and 1 or-1;
else
    if H~=nil then
        U=math.atan2(p(q-H),x(q-H));
        X=if T(U)>1.0E-4 then
            U>0 and 1 or-1
        else
            X;
        else
        end;
    end;
    if X then
    else
        U=tonumber(f);
        X=if U then
            Y(U)%2==0 and 1 or-1
        else
            -(d.strafe_side or 1);
        end;
        d.strafe_side=X;
        return O(Q*X,q);
    end;
    local function Y(X)if typeof(X)~="Vector3"then return nil;
elseif  T(X.Y)>=0.7 then
    return nil;
end;
local q=Vector3.new(X.X,0,X.Z);
if q.Magnitude<0.3 then
    return nil;
end;
return q.Unit;
end;
local function X(q)return q.Stance=="Ducking"and"Ducking"or"Standing";
end;
local function q(U,f)local Q=f=="Ducking"and U.PlayerSizeDucking or U.PlayerSizeStanding;
return typeof(Q)=="Vector3"and Q.X*0.5 or 1.2;
end;
local function U(f,Q)local c=Q=="Ducking"and f.PlayerSizeDucking or f.PlayerSizeStanding;
return typeof(c)=="Vector3"and c.Y*0.5 or 2.7;
end;
local function f(Q,c)return c.Position.Y-U(Q,X(c));
end;
local function Q(c)local H,s=c.PlayerSizeStanding,c.PlayerSizeDucking;
if typeof(H)~="Vector3"then return 0.27;
elseif  typeof(s)~="Vector3"then return 0.27;
end;
return(H.Y-s.Y)*(tonumber(c.DuckJumpOriginShiftFraction)or 0.2);
end;
local function c(H)local s=tonumber(H.DuckAmount);
if s then
    if s>=0.999 then
        return true;
    end;
end;
s=tonumber(H.DuckCooldownSeconds);
if s then
    if s>0 then
        local s=tonumber(H.DuckAmount);
        if s then
            if s<=0.75 then
                return false;
            end;
        else
            return false;
        end;
    end;
end;
return(tonumber(H.DuckSpeed)or 8)>=3.5;
end;
local function H(s,F,v,u)local V,e=pcall(s.Sweep,s,F,v,u,nil,1,1);
if V then
    if type(e)=="table"then return e;
end;
end;
return nil;
end;
local function s(F,v,u,V)local e,l=pcall(F.FindGround,F,v,u,V,1);
if e then
    if type(l)=="table"then return l;
end;
end;
return nil;
end;
local function F(v,u,V)local e,l=pcall(v.IsClear,v,u,V,1);
return e and l==true;
end;
local function v(u,V)if V<=0 then
    return 0;
end;
local e=z(tonumber(u.Friction)or 5.2,0.1);
local l=(tonumber(u.GroundAccelerate)or 5.5)*z(tonumber(u.DefaultBaseMoveSpeed)or 18.75,1)/e;
u=J(1+V/l)/e;
return(V+l)/e*(1-y(-e*u))-l*u;
end;
local function J(y,u,V,e,l)local C=u-Vector3.new(0,0.5,0);
u=C+V*(e*2+0.35);
if not(F(y,u,l))then
    return V;
end;
e=H(y,u,C,l);
if e then
    if not(e.StartSolid)then
        C=tonumber(e.Fraction);
        if C then
            if C<1 then
                u=Y(e.Normal);
                if u then
                    if u:Dot(V)>0.5 then
                        return u;
                    end;
                end;
            end;
        end;
    end;
end;
return V;
end;
local function y(u)return(tonumber(u.MaxStepHeight)or 1.35)+0.15;
end;
local function u(V,e,l,C)return F(V,e,l)and s(V,e,l,C)==nil;
end;
local function V(e,l,C)return s(e,l,C,1.5)~=nil;
end;
local function e(l)w.mode=nil;
w.normal=nil;
w.duck=false;
w.wide=false;
w.ticks=0;
w.check=0;
w.away=0;
if l then
    w.rest=10;
end;
end;
local function l(C,W)local mF,DF,jF,hF=C.world.Composed.Query,C.config,X(W),W.Position;
local bF,KF,SF=q(DF,jF),y(DF),w.mode=="catch"and w.normal or nil;
e(false);
SF=SF and(if u(mF,hF+SF*(bF*2+0.6),jF,KF)then
    SF
else
    nil);
    if SF then
    else
        C,DF=bF*2+0.35,Vector3.zero;
        for oF=1,#g,1 do
            W=g[oF];
            DF=if u(mF,hF+W*C,jF,KF)then
                DF+W
            else
                DF;
            end;
            SF=if DF.Magnitude>0.3 then
                J(mF,hF,DF.Unit,bF,jF)
            else
                SF;
            end;
            if not(SF)then
                return;
            end;
            w.mode="ride";
            w.normal=SF;
            w.wide=not u(mF,hF-SF*(bF*2+0.35),jF,KF);
        end;
        local function J(C,W,mF,DF,jF,hF,bF)local KF,SF,oF=C.world.Composed.Query,C.config,w.normal;
        local tF,GF=Vector3.new(-oF.Z,0,oF.X),A(mF,DF);
        mF=GF:Dot(oF);
        if mF>0.7 then
            w.away=w.away+1;
            if w.away>=30 then
                e(true);
                return nil;
            end;
        else
            w.away=0;
        end;
        local iF=X(W);
        w.check=w.check+1;
        if w.check>=8 then
            w.check=0;
            if not(u(KF,W.Position+oF*(q(SF,iF)*2+0.6),iF,y(SF)))then
                e(false);
                return nil;
            end;
        end;
        local y,u,YF,OF,TF=_(_(hF,R,false),n,false),GF:Dot(tF),bF:Dot(oF),bF:Dot(tF),false;
        GF=v(SF,T(OF))+0.45;
        if T(OF)>0.5 and not V(KF,W.Position+tF*(OF>0 and GF or-GF),iF)then
            u,TF=OF>0 and-1 or 1,true;
        else
            if T(u)>0.05 and(u*OF<=0 or T(OF)<=0.5)then
                if V(KF,W.Position+tF*(u>0 and GF or-GF),iF)then
                else
                    bF,hF=true,0;
                    u,TF=hF,bF;
                end;
            else
            end;
        end;
        bF=if w.wide then
            z(YF,0)*0.9+z(-mF,0)
        else
            YF*0.9;
            for OF=1,3,1 do
                iF=if OF==1 then
                    tF*u-oF*i(bF,-1,1)
                else
                    if OF==2 then
                        tF*(u*0.5)-oF*(YF>=0 and 1 or-1)
                    else
                        Vector3.zero;
                        iF=if iF.Magnitude>1 then
                            iF.Unit
                        else
                            iF;
                            mF=iF.Magnitude>0.01 and O(iF,DF)or Vector2.zero;
                            if OF==1 then
                                if not(TF)then
                                    if YF<=0 then
                                        if iF:Dot(oF)<=0 then
                                            return mF;
                                        end;
                                    end;
                                end;
                            end;
                            W=P(C,mF,DF,jF,y);
                            if W then
                                if W.OnGround==true then
                                    hF=W.Velocity;
                                    GF=hF.X*oF.X+hF.Z*oF.Z;
                                    if GF<=0.35 then
                                        return mF;
                                    elseif  V(KF,W.Position+oF*v(SF,GF),X(W))then
                                        return mF;
                                    end;
                                end;
                            end;
                        end;
                        e(true);
                        return nil;
                    end;
                    local function y(v,u,V,C)local W=Vector3.new(V.X,0,V.Z);
                    local mF,DF,jF,hF=W.Magnitude>1 and W.Unit or nil,v.world.Composed.Query,X(u),u.Position;
                    V=if mF then
                        C+mF*0.6
                    else
                        C;
                        if V.Magnitude<0.15 then
                            return nil;
                        end;
                        W=Y(u.WallNormal);
                        u,C=W and-V.Unit:Dot(W)or nil,W;
                        for bF=1,#g,1 do
                            mF=g[bF];
                            if V:Dot(mF)>0.15 then
                                v=H(DF,hF,hF+mF*2.6,jF);
                                if v then
                                    if v.AllSolid then
                                    else
                                        bF=tonumber(v.Fraction);
                                        if bF then
                                            if bF<1 then
                                                W=Y(v.Normal);
                                                if W then
                                                    local Y=-V.Unit:Dot(W);
                                                    if Y>0.25 then
                                                        if u then
                                                            if Y>u then
                                                                u,C=Y,W;
                                                            else
                                                            end;
                                                        else
                                                            u,C=Y,W;
                                                        end;
                                                    else
                                                    end;
                                                else
                                                end;
                                            else
                                            end;
                                        else
                                        end;
                                    end;
                                else
                                end;
                            else
                            end;
                        end;
                        return C;
                    end;
                    local function Y(g,H,v,u)local V,C,W,mF=g.world.Composed.Query,g.config,H.Position,X(H);
                    local X,DF=f(C,H),T(tonumber(C.Gravity)or 60);
                    local f,jF,hF,bF,KF=u.Y>0 and u.Y*u.Y/(2*DF)or 0,z(0,tonumber(C.AutoJumpLedgeAssistHeight)or 0),U(C,mF),(tonumber(H.DuckAmount)or 0)>=0.999,0;
                    if bF then
                    else
                        KF=Q(C);
                        KF=if mF~="Ducking"then KF+(C.PlayerSizeStanding.Y-C.PlayerSizeDucking.Y)*0.5
                    else
                        KF;
                    end;
                    g=s(V,W,mF,14);
                    DF,u=g and g.Position.Y-hF or X-14,X+f;
                    f,g=u+jF;
                    for U=1,3,1 do
                        X=W-v*(U==1 and 0.1 or U==2 and 0.4 or q(C,mF)+0.2);
                        for q=1,#k,1 do
                            jF=f+KF*k[q]+0.2;
                            U=Vector3.new(X.X,jF+hF,X.Z);
                            if F(V,U,mF)then
                                q=s(V,U,mF,jF-DF+14);
                                if q then
                                    g=q.Position.Y-hF;
                                    break;
                                end;
                            end;
                        end;
                        if g then
                            break;
                        end;
                    end;
                    if g then
                        if g<=DF+0.6 then
                            return nil;
                        end;
                    else
                        return nil;
                    end;
                    if g<=u-0.2 then
                        return bF;
                    end;
                    if KF>0 then
                        if g<=f+KF then
                            if c(H)then
                                return true;
                            end;
                        end;
                    end;
                    if g<=f then
                        return bF;
                    end;
                    return nil;
                end;
                local function X(q,U,z,f,k,g)local Q,c=A(z,f),w.mode=="catch"and w.normal or nil;
                if c then
                    w.ticks=w.ticks+1;
                    if w.ticks>40 then
                        e(false);
                        c=nil;
                    else
                        if Q:Dot(c)>0.7 then
                            e(false);
                            c=nil;
                        else
                        end;
                    end;
                else
                end;
                z=c;
                if not z then
                    c=y(q,U,k,Q);
                    if not(c)then
                        return nil;
                    end;
                    local A=Y(q,U,c,k);
                    if A==nil then
                        return nil;
                    end;
                    w.mode="catch";
                    w.normal=c;
                    w.duck=A;
                    w.ticks=0;
                    w.check=0;
                    return O(-c,f),A or g;
                end;
                w.check=w.check+1;
                if w.check>=3 then
                    w.check=0;
                    Q=Y(q,U,z,k);
                    if Q==nil then
                        e(false);
                        return nil;
                    end;
                    if Q then
                        w.duck=true;
                    end;
                end;
                return O(-z,f),w.duck or g;
            end;
            local function Y(A,q,U,z,y,f,k,g,Q,c,H)if g then
                if H then
                    e(true);
                    return nil;
                end;
                if Q or w.mode=="catch"then if w.rest>0 then
                    e(false);
                    return nil;
                elseif  c then
                    e(false);
                    return nil;
                end;
                l(A,q);
            end;
            if w.mode~="ride"then return nil;
        end;
        local g=J(A,q,U,z,y,f,k);
        if g then
            return g,false;
        end;
        return nil;
    end;
    if w.mode=="ride"then e(false);
end;
if w.rest>0 then
    return nil;
end;
return X(A,q,U,z,k,B(f,n)~=0);
end;
local function A(X)d.char=X;
d.bug_pending=false;
d.hz=nil;
d.config=nil;
d.strafe_yaw=nil;
d.strafe_side=1;
w.mode=nil;
w.normal=nil;
w.duck=false;
w.wide=false;
w.ticks=0;
w.check=0;
w.away=0;
w.rest=0;
w.jump=false;
w.air=false;
end;
local function X(q,U,z)local J=U and U.State or q and q.MovementState;
if type(J)~="table"then return nil;
end;
local y=q and q.Character;
if d.char~=y then
    A(y);
end;
local f,k,g,Q,c,H,s,F,v=tonumber(z.Buttons)or 0,typeof(z.Move)=="Vector2"and z.Move or Vector2.zero,tonumber(z.LookYaw)or tonumber(J.LookYaw)or 0,tonumber(z.VerticalLook)or tonumber(J.VerticalLook)or 0,typeof(J.Velocity)=="Vector3"and J.Velocity or q and q.GlobalVelocity or Vector3.zero,J.OnGround==true,J.MovementMode==nil or J.MovementMode=="Walking";
y=false;
if not(I.jump_bug)then
    d.bug_pending=false;
end;
if s and I.strafe then
    if H then
        d.strafe_yaw=nil;
    end;
else
    d.strafe_yaw=nil;
end;
local u=B(f,R)~=0;
local V,l=u and not w.jump,w.air and H;
w.jump=u;
local C=not H;
w.air=C;
local W=I.pixel_surf and s;
if W then
    if w.rest>0 then
        w.rest=w.rest-1;
    end;
elseif  w.mode~=nil then
    e(false);
end;
local w=if s and I.bhop then
    if q then
        if q.JumpInputDown then
            H
        else
            nil
        else
            nil
        else
            nil;
            if d.bug_pending then
                d.bug_pending=false;
                if I.jump_bug then
                    if s then
                        if H then
                            local e,mF,DF=true,false,true;
                            w,F,y=e,mF,DF;
                        else
                        end;
                    else
                    end;
                else
                end;
            else
            end;
            q=s and(W or I.edge_jump and H or(if H then
                C
            else
                I.jump_bug or I.edge_bug))and Z(U,J)or nil;
                if q and not y then
                    if W then
                        local e,mF=Y(q,J,k,g,Q,f,c,H,l,u,V);
                        if e then
                            local Y,DF,jF=false,mF==true,true;
                            w,F,v,y=Y,DF,e,jF;
                        else
                        end;
                    else
                    end;
                else
                end;
                if q and not y then
                    if I.edge_jump then
                        if H then
                            V,u,J=P(q,k,g,Q,_(f,R,false));
                            w=if V and J.LeftGround then
                                if J.Jumped then
                                    w
                                else
                                    if V.OnGround~=true then
                                        true
                                    else
                                        w
                                    else
                                        w;
                                    else
                                    end;
                                else
                                end;
                            else
                            end;
                            if q and not y and I.edge_bug and C then
                                if c.Y<-4 then
                                    W=if w~=nil then
                                        (_(f,R,w))
                                    else
                                        f;
                                        W=if F~=nil then
                                            (_(W,n,F))
                                        else
                                            W;
                                            l,J=r(q,k,g,Q,W);
                                            if typeof(l)=="Vector2"then local Y,r,u=false,J==true,true;
                                            w,F,v,y=Y,r,l,u;
                                        else
                                        end;
                                    else
                                    end;
                                else
                                end;
                                if q and not y and I.jump_bug and C then
                                    if c.Y<-4 then
                                        l=_(_(f,R,false),n,true);
                                        V,J,W=P(q,k,g,Q,l);
                                        if V then
                                            if W.Landed then
                                                d.bug_pending=true;
                                                local Y,d,r=false,true,true;
                                                w,F,y=Y,d,r;
                                            else
                                            end;
                                        else
                                        end;
                                    else
                                    end;
                                else
                                end;
                                v=if(if y then
                                    not y
                                else
                                    s)and I.strafe then
                                        if H then
                                            v
                                        else
                                            L(k,g,c,U and U.ServerTick)
                                        else
                                            v;
                                            if typeof(v)=="Vector2"then z.Move=v;
                                        end;
                                        f=if w~=nil then
                                            (_(f,R,w))
                                        else
                                            f;
                                            f=if F~=nil then
                                                (_(f,n,F))
                                            else
                                                f;
                                                z.Buttons=if I.slow_walk and s then
                                                    if H then
                                                        _(f,E,true)
                                                    else
                                                        f
                                                    else
                                                        f;
                                                        return F;
                                                    end;
                                                    local function Y(n,E,d)if typeof(n.Move)~="Vector2"then return;
                                                end;
                                                local w=E-d;
                                                if T(w)<=1.0E-6 then
                                                    return;
                                                end;
                                                local T,E,d=x(w),p(w),n.Move;
                                                n.Move=Vector2.new(d.X*T-d.Y*E,d.X*E+d.Y*T);
                                            end;
                                            local function T(x)if x>0.1 then
                                                return 1;
                                            elseif  x<-0.1 then
                                                return-1;
                                            end;
                                            return 0;
                                        end;
                                        local function x(p)if typeof(p)~="Vector3"then return 0;
                                    end;
                                    return Vector3.new(p.X,0,p.Z).Magnitude;
                                end;
                                local function p(n)local E=n.CameraPart;
                                if typeof(E)=="Instance"then if E:IsA("BasePart")then
                                    if E.Parent then
                                        return E.Position;
                                    end;
                                end;
                            end;
                            E=n.HumanoidRootPart;
                            if typeof(E)=="Instance"then if E:IsA("BasePart")then
                                if E.Parent then
                                    return E.Position+Vector3.new(0,2,0);
                                end;
                            end;
                        end;
                        return nil;
                    end;
                    local function n(E,d,w,r,L)local q=d.Velocity;
                    if typeof(q)~="Vector3"then return Vector2.zero;
                elseif  d.OnGround~=true then
                    return Vector2.zero;
                end;
                local U=Vector3.new(q.X,0,q.Z);
                q=U.Magnitude;
                if q<0.05 then
                    return Vector2.zero;
                end;
                local z,J=O(-U/q,w),Z(E,d);
                if J then
                    U,E=P(J,Vector2.zero,w,r,L),P(J,z,w,r,L);
                    if U then
                        if E then
                            if x(E.Velocity)<x(U.Velocity)then
                                return z;
                            end;
                            return Vector2.zero;
                        end;
                    end;
                end;
                if q>2.5 then
                    return z;
                end;
                return Vector2.zero;
            end;
            local function O(E,d,w,P,r,L)local Z=os.clock();
            if P.Magnitude>0.1 then
                o.input_at=Z;
            elseif  r then
                o.input_at=Z;
            end;
            if E then
                if o.broke then
                    return;
                elseif  not(o.active(Z))then
                    return;
                end;
            else
                return;
            end;
            local q,U=T(P.X),T(P.Y);
            if o.fresh then
                o.fresh=false;
                o.bx=q;
                o.by=U;
                o.bj=r;
            elseif  q~=0 and q~=o.bx or U~=0 and U~=o.by then
                o.broke=true;
                return;
            elseif  r then
                if not(o.bj)then
                    o.broke=true;
                    return;
